// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";

// Interfaces de DEX Routers en Arbitrum One
interface IUniswapV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface ICamelotV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 limitSqrtPrice;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface ICamelotV2Router {
    function swapExactTokensForTokens(
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint256 amountOutMin,
        address[] calldata path,
        address to,
        uint256 deadline
    ) external returns (uint256[] memory amounts);
}

// Aave V3 Flash Loan Interfaces
interface IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver {
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool);
}

interface IPool {
    function flashLoanSimple(
        address receiverAddress,
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        bytes calldata params,
        uint16 referralCode
    ) external;
}

// Balancer V2 Flash Loan Interfaces (0% Fee)
interface IBalancerVault {
    function flashLoan(
        address recipient,
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

interface IFlashLoanRecipient {
    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

contract ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage is IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver, IFlashLoanRecipient {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    // Constantes de Red en Arbitrum One (ChainID 42161)
    address public constant WETH = 0x82aF49447D8a07e3bd95BD0d56f35241523fBab1;
    address public constant AAVE_POOL = 0x794a61358D6845594F94dc1DB02A252b5b4814aD;
    address public constant BALANCER_VAULT = 0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8;

    // DEX Routers en Arbitrum
    address public constant UNIV3_ROUTER = 0x68b3465833fb72A70ecDF485E0e4C7bD8665Fc45;
    address public constant CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER = 0x1F721E2E82F6676FCE4eA07A5958cF098D339e18;
    address public constant CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER = 0xc873fEcbd354f5A56E00E710B90EF4201db2448d;
    address public constant PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER = 0x1b81D678ffb9C0263b24A97847620C99d213eB14;
    address public constant SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER = 0xFB7eF66a7e61224DD6FcD0D7d9C3be5C8B049b9f;

    // Enum de DEXes en Arbitrum
    enum DexType {
        UNIV3,          // 0: Uniswap V3 (Concentrated Liquidity)
        CAMELOT_V3,     // 1: Camelot V3 / Algebra (CL)
        CAMELOT_V2,     // 2: Camelot Classic AMM V2
        PANCAKE_V3,     // 3: PancakeSwap V3 (CL)
        SUSHISWAP_V3    // 4: SushiSwap V3 (CL)
    }

    struct SpatialRoute {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenIntermediate;
        uint8 dexBuy;
        uint8 dexSell;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingBuy;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingSell;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    struct TriangularRoute {
        address tokenA;
        address tokenB;
        address tokenC;
        uint8 dex1;
        uint8 dex2;
        uint8 dex3;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing1;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing2;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing3;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    address public owner;

    // â”€â”€ Variables de Retorno y Seguridad de Callbacks â”€â”€
    uint256 private _lastProfit;
    bool private _inFlashLoan;

    event SpatialExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 amountIn, uint256 amountOut, uint256 profit);
    event TriangularExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 amountIn, uint256 amountOut, uint256 profit);
    event FlashLoanExecuted(address indexed asset, uint256 loanAmount, uint256 premium, uint256 netProfit);

    error OnlyOwner();
    error NotAavePool();
    error NotBalancerVault();
    error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required);
    error InvalidDEX(uint8 dexId);

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert OnlyOwner();
        _;
    }

    constructor() {
        owner = msg.sender;
        _approveTokenToRouters(WETH);
    }

    receive() external payable {}

    function _approveTokenToRouters(address token) internal {
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(UNIV3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
    }

    function approveRouters(address[] calldata tokens) external onlyOwner {
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            _approveTokenToRouters(tokens[i]);
        }
    }

    function _swap(
        address tokenIn,
        address tokenOut,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint8 dexId,
        uint24 feeOrSpacing
    ) internal returns (uint256 amountOut) {
        address router;
        if (dexId == uint8(DexType.UNIV3)) router = UNIV3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V3)) router = CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V2)) router = CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.PANCAKE_V3)) router = PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.SUSHISWAP_V3)) router = SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER;
        else revert InvalidDEX(dexId);

        if (IERC20(tokenIn).allowance(address(this), router) < amountIn) {
            IERC20(tokenIn).forceApprove(router, type(uint256).max);
        }

        if (dexId == uint8(DexType.UNIV3) || dexId == uint8(DexType.PANCAKE_V3) || dexId == uint8(DexType.SUSHISWAP_V3)) {
            IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams memory params = IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            });
            return IUniswapV3Router(router).exactInputSingle(params);
        } else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V3)) {
            ICamelotV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams memory params = ICamelotV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                limitSqrtPrice: 0
            });
            return ICamelotV3Router(router).exactInputSingle(params);
        } else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V2)) {
            address[] memory path = new address[](2);
            path[0] = tokenIn;
            path[1] = tokenOut;
            uint256[] memory amounts = ICamelotV2Router(router).swapExactTokensForTokens(
                amountIn,
                0,
                path,
                address(this),
                block.timestamp
            );
            return amounts[amounts.length - 1];
        }
        revert InvalidDEX(dexId);
    }

    function executeSpotSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 amountIn) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        IERC20(route.tokenIn).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amountIn);
        uint256 initialBalance = IERC20(route.tokenIn).balanceOf(address(this));

        uint256 interAmount = _swap(route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amountIn, route.dexBuy, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
        uint256 finalOut = _swap(route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, interAmount, route.dexSell, route.feeOrSpacingSell);

        uint256 finalBalance = IERC20(route.tokenIn).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredAmount = initialBalance + route.minProfit;

        if (finalBalance < requiredAmount) {
            revert NotProfitable(finalBalance, requiredAmount);
        }

        profit = finalBalance - initialBalance;
        IERC20(route.tokenIn).safeTransfer(msg.sender, amountIn + profit);

        emit SpatialExecuted(route.tokenIn, amountIn, finalOut, profit);
        return profit;
    }

    function executeSpotTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 amountIn) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amountIn);
        uint256 initialBalance = IERC20(route.tokenA).balanceOf(address(this));

        uint256 amountB = _swap(route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amountIn, route.dex1, route.feeOrSpacing1);
        uint256 amountC = _swap(route.tokenB, route.tokenC, amountB, route.dex2, route.feeOrSpacing2);
        uint256 finalOut = _swap(route.tokenC, route.tokenA, amountC, route.dex3, route.feeOrSpacing3);

        uint256 finalBalance = IERC20(route.tokenA).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredAmount = initialBalance + route.minProfit;

        if (finalBalance < requiredAmount) {
            revert NotProfitable(finalBalance, requiredAmount);
        }

        profit = finalBalance - initialBalance;
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransfer(msg.sender, amountIn + profit);

        emit TriangularExecuted(route.tokenA, amountIn, finalOut, profit);
        return profit;
    }

    function executeFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(0), abi.encode(route));
        IPool(AAVE_POOL).flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenIn, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(1), abi.encode(route));
        IPool(AAVE_POOL).flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenA, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenIn);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(0), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeBalancerFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenA);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(1), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external override returns (bool) {
        if (msg.sender != AAVE_POOL) revert NotAavePool();
        require(initiator == address(this), "Initiator not this contract");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        (uint8 routeType, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(params, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 amountOwed = amount + premium;

        if (routeType == 0) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            uint256 interAmount = _swap(route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.dexBuy, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, interAmount, route.dexSell, route.feeOrSpacingSell);

            uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
            if (currentBalance < amountOwed + route.minProfit) {
                revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, amountOwed + route.minProfit);
            }

            IERC20(asset).safeIncreaseAllowance(AAVE_POOL, amountOwed);
            uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - amountOwed;
            _lastProfit = netProfit;
            if (netProfit > 0) {
                IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
            }
            emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, premium, netProfit);
        } else if (routeType == 1) {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            uint256 amountB = _swap(route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amount, route.dex1, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 amountC = _swap(route.tokenB, route.tokenC, amountB, route.dex2, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.tokenC, route.tokenA, amountC, route.dex3, route.feeOrSpacing3);

            uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
            if (currentBalance < amountOwed + route.minProfit) {
                revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, amountOwed + route.minProfit);
            }

            IERC20(asset).safeIncreaseAllowance(AAVE_POOL, amountOwed);
            uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - amountOwed;
            _lastProfit = netProfit;
            if (netProfit > 0) {
                IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
            }
            emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, premium, netProfit);
        }

        return true;
    }

    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external override {
        if (msg.sender != BALANCER_VAULT) revert NotBalancerVault();
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        (uint8 routeType, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(userData, (uint8, bytes));
        address asset = address(tokens[0]);
        uint256 amount = amounts[0];
        uint256 amountOwed = amount + feeAmounts[0]; // Balancer fee is 0%

        if (routeType == 0) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            uint256 interAmount = _swap(route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.dexBuy, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, interAmount, route.dexSell, route.feeOrSpacingSell);

            uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
            if (currentBalance < amountOwed + route.minProfit) {
                revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, amountOwed + route.minProfit);
            }

            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(BALANCER_VAULT, amountOwed);
            uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - amountOwed;
            _lastProfit = netProfit;
            if (netProfit > 0) {
                IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
            }
            emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, feeAmounts[0], netProfit);
        } else if (routeType == 1) {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            uint256 amountB = _swap(route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amount, route.dex1, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 amountC = _swap(route.tokenB, route.tokenC, amountB, route.dex2, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.tokenC, route.tokenA, amountC, route.dex3, route.feeOrSpacing3);

            uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
            if (currentBalance < amountOwed + route.minProfit) {
                revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, amountOwed + route.minProfit);
            }

            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(BALANCER_VAULT, amountOwed);
            uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - amountOwed;
            _lastProfit = netProfit;
            if (netProfit > 0) {
                IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
            }
            emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, feeAmounts[0], netProfit);
        }
    }

    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner {
        uint256 balance = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (balance > 0) {
            IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, balance);
        }
    }

    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner {
        uint256 balance = address(this).balance;
        if (balance > 0) {
            (bool s, ) = payable(owner).call{value: balance}("");
            require(s, "ETH Transfer failed");
        }
    }
}

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(
        ExactInputSingleParams calldata params
    ) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        int24 tickSpacing;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(
        ExactInputSingleParams calldata params
    ) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IPool {

        address receiverAddress,
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        bytes calldata params,
        uint16 referralCode
    ) external;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  Contrato Principal: Arbitraje AtÃ³mico Multi-DEX en Base
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

contract BaseMultiDexArbitrage {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    struct TriangularRoute {
        address tokenA;
        address tokenB;
        address tokenC;
        uint8 dex1;
        uint8 dex2;
        uint8 dex3;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing1;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing2;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing3;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    address public immutable owner;

    // â”€â”€ Routers en Base (Inmutables) â”€â”€
    IUniswapV3Router public constant UNIV3_ROUTER =
        IUniswapV3Router(0x2626664c2603336E57B271c5C0b26F421741e481);

    IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter public constant AERO_CL_ROUTER =
        IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter(0xBE6D8f0d05cC4be24d5167a3eF062215bE6D18a5);

    IPool public constant AAVE_POOL =
        IPool(0xA238Dd80C259a72e81d7e4664a9801593F98d1c5);

    // â”€â”€ Identificadores de DEX â”€â”€
    uint8 public constant DEX_UNIV3 = 0;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_CL = 1;

    // â”€â”€ Reentrancy Guard Gas-Efficient â”€â”€
    uint256 private constant _NOT_ENTERED = 1;
    uint256 private constant _ENTERED = 2;
    uint256 private _status = _NOT_ENTERED;

    // â”€â”€ Errores Personalizados â”€â”€
    error NotOwner();
    error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required);
    error Reentrancy();
    error SwapFailed(uint8 leg);

    event TriangularExecuted(
        address indexed tokenStart,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint256 amountOut,
        uint256 profit
    );
    event FlashLoanExecuted(
        address indexed asset,
        uint256 loanAmount,
        uint256 premium,
        uint256 netProfit
    );

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();
        _;
    }

    modifier nonReentrant() {
        if (_status == _ENTERED) revert Reentrancy();
        _status = _ENTERED;
        _;
        _status = _NOT_ENTERED;
    }

    constructor() {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  MODO 1: Arbitraje Spot AtÃ³mico con Saldo Propio ($35 WETH)
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

    /**
     * @notice Ejecuta arbitraje triangular atÃ³mico con saldo spot.
     *         Si out3 < amountIn + minProfit, la transacciÃ³n revierte por completo (0 pÃ©rdidas).
     */
    function executeSpotTriangular(
        TriangularRoute calldata route,
        uint256 amountIn
    ) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
        // 1. Transferir tokens del caller al contrato
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amountIn);

        // 2. Ejecutar los 3 saltos secuencialmente
        uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amountIn, route.feeOrSpacing1);
        uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
        uint256 out3 = _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, route.tokenA, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);

        // 3. Verificar rentabilidad estricta
        if (out3 < amountIn + route.minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(out3, amountIn + route.minProfit);
        }

        // 4. Devolver capital + ganancias al owner
        uint256 finalBalance = IERC20(route.tokenA).balanceOf(address(this));
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransfer(owner, finalBalance);

        emit TriangularExecuted(route.tokenA, amountIn, out3, out3 - amountIn);
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  MODO 2: Arbitraje con Flash Loan de Aave V3 ($0 Capital)
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

    /**
     * @notice Inicia un flash loan de Aave V3 y ejecuta arbitraje triangular multi-DEX.
     */
    function executeFlashLoanTriangular(
        TriangularRoute calldata route,
        uint256 loanAmount
    ) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(route);

        AAVE_POOL.flashLoanSimple(
            address(this),
            route.tokenA,
            loanAmount,
            params,
            0
        );
    }

    /**
     * @notice Callback de Aave V3 ejecutado automÃ¡ticamente al recibir el flash loan.
     */
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool) {
        require(msg.sender == address(AAVE_POOL), "Caller not Aave Pool");
        require(initiator == address(this), "Initiator not self");

        TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(params, (TriangularRoute));

        // Ejecutar los 3 saltos
        uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, asset, route.tokenB, amount, route.feeOrSpacing1);
        uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
        uint256 out3 = _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, asset, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);

        uint256 totalOwed = amount + premium;
        if (out3 < totalOwed + route.minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(out3, totalOwed + route.minProfit);
        }

        // Aprobar repago exacto a Aave Pool
        IERC20(asset).forceApprove(address(AAVE_POOL), totalOwed);

        // Transferir ganancia neta al owner
        uint256 profit = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this)) - totalOwed;
        if (profit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, profit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, premium, profit);
        return true;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  LÃ³gica de Swap Interna Multi-DEX
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

    function _swap(
        uint8 dex,
        address tokenIn,
        address tokenOut,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint24 feeOrSpacing
    ) internal returns (uint256 amountOut) {
        address router = dex == DEX_UNIV3 ? address(UNIV3_ROUTER) : address(AERO_CL_ROUTER);
        
        // Garantizar aprobaciÃ³n del router si la asignaciÃ³n es insuficiente
        if (IERC20(tokenIn).allowance(address(this), router) < amountIn) {
            IERC20(tokenIn).forceApprove(router, type(uint256).max);
        }

        if (dex == DEX_UNIV3) {
            amountOut = UNIV3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(
                IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                    tokenIn: tokenIn,
                    tokenOut: tokenOut,
                    fee: feeOrSpacing,
                    recipient: address(this),
                    amountIn: amountIn,
                    amountOutMinimum: 0,
                    sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
                })
            );
        } else if (dex == DEX_AERO_CL) {
            amountOut = AERO_CL_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(
                IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter.ExactInputSingleParams({
                    tokenIn: tokenIn,
                    tokenOut: tokenOut,
                    tickSpacing: int24(uint24(feeOrSpacing)),
                    recipient: address(this),
                    deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                    amountIn: amountIn,
                    amountOutMinimum: 0,
                    sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
                })
            );
        } else {
            revert SwapFailed(dex);
        }

        if (amountOut == 0) revert SwapFailed(dex);
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  AdministraciÃ³n y Pre-Aprobaciones
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

    /**
     * @notice Pre-aprueba una lista de tokens a los routers para maximizar ahorro de gas en swaps.
     */
    function approveRouters(address[] calldata tokens) external onlyOwner {
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(UNIV3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(AERO_CL_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
        }
    }

    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (bal > 0) IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, bal);
    }

    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = address(this).balance;
        if (bal > 0) {
            (bool ok, ) = owner.call{value: bal}("");
            require(ok, "ETH transfer failed");
        }
    }

    receive() external payable {}
}

interface IUniswapV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        int24 tickSpacing;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IAerodromeV2Router {
    struct Route {
        address from;
        address to;
        bool stable;
        address factory;
    }
    function swapExactTokensForTokens(
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint256 amountOutMin,
        Route[] calldata routes,
        address to,
        uint256 deadline
    ) external returns (uint256[] memory amounts);
}

        uint16 referralCode
    ) external;

interface IFlashLoanRecipient {

}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  Contrato Universal de Arbitraje en Base
//  Soporta Spot Directo, Aave V3 y Balancer V2 (0% Fee)
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

contract BaseUniversalArbitrage is IFlashLoanRecipient {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    address public owner;

    // â”€â”€ Direcciones Inmutables en Base Mainnet (ChainID 8453) â”€â”€
    address public constant WETH = 0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006;
    address public constant USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;

    IUniswapV3Router public constant UNIV3_ROUTER = IUniswapV3Router(0x2626664c2603336E57B271c5C0b26F421741e481);
    IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter public constant AERO_CL_ROUTER = IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter(0xBE6D8f0d05cC4be24d5167a3eF062215bE6D18a5);
    IAerodromeV2Router public constant AERO_V2_ROUTER = IAerodromeV2Router(0xcF77a3Ba9A5CA399B7c97c74d54e5b1Beb874E43);
    address public constant AERO_V2_FACTORY = 0x420DD381b31aEf6683db6B902084cB0FFECe40Da;
    IUniswapV3Router public constant PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER = IUniswapV3Router(0x1b81D678ffb9C0263b24A97847620C99d213eB14);
    IUniswapV3Router public constant SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER = IUniswapV3Router(0xFB7eF66a7e61224DD6FcD0D7d9C3be5C8B049b9f);
    IPool public constant AAVE_POOL = IPool(0xA238Dd80C259a72e81d7e4664a9801593F98d1c5);
    address public constant BALANCER_VAULT = 0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8;

    // â”€â”€ Identificadores de DEX â”€â”€
    uint8 public constant DEX_UNIV3 = 0;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_CL = 1;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_V2_VOLATILE = 2;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE = 3;
    uint8 public constant DEX_PANCAKE_V3 = 4;
    uint8 public constant DEX_SUSHISWAP_V3 = 5;

    // â”€â”€ Reentrancy Guard Gas-Efficient â”€â”€
    uint256 private constant _NOT_ENTERED = 1;
    uint256 private constant _ENTERED = 2;
    uint256 private _status = _NOT_ENTERED;

    // â”€â”€ Variables de Retorno y Seguridad de Callbacks â”€â”€
    uint256 private _lastProfit;
    bool private _inFlashLoan;

    struct SpatialRoute {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenIntermediate;
        uint8 dexBuy;
        uint8 dexSell;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingBuy;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingSell;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    struct TriangularRoute {
        address tokenA;
        address tokenB;
        address tokenC;
        uint8 dex1;
        uint8 dex2;
        uint8 dex3;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing1;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing2;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing3;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    // â”€â”€ Errores Personalizados â”€â”€
    error NotOwner();
    error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required);
    error Reentrancy();
    error SwapFailed(uint8 dex);

    // â”€â”€ Eventos con TelemetrÃ­a On-Chain â”€â”€
    event SpatialExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 amountIn, uint256 amountOut, uint256 profit);
    event TriangularExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 amountIn, uint256 amountOut, uint256 profit);
    event FlashLoanExecuted(address indexed asset, uint256 loanAmount, uint256 premium, uint256 netProfit);

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();
        _;
    }

    modifier nonReentrant() {
        if (_status == _ENTERED) revert Reentrancy();
        _status = _ENTERED;
        _;
        _status = _NOT_ENTERED;
    }

    constructor() {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  1. Arbitraje Espacial Directo 2-Leg Spot
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    function executeSpotSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 amountIn) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        IERC20(route.tokenIn).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amountIn);

        uint256 intermediateAmount = _swap(route.dexBuy, route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amountIn, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
        uint256 finalAmount = _swap(route.dexSell, route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, intermediateAmount, route.feeOrSpacingSell);

        if (finalAmount < amountIn + route.minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(finalAmount, amountIn + route.minProfit);
        }

        profit = finalAmount - amountIn;
        IERC20(route.tokenIn).safeTransfer(owner, finalAmount);
        emit SpatialExecuted(route.tokenIn, amountIn, finalAmount, profit);
        return profit;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  2. Arbitraje Triangular 3-Leg Spot
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    function executeSpotTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 amountIn) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amountIn);

        uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amountIn, route.feeOrSpacing1);
        uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
        uint256 out3 = _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, route.tokenA, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);

        if (out3 < amountIn + route.minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(out3, amountIn + route.minProfit);
        }

        profit = out3 - amountIn;
        IERC20(route.tokenA).safeTransfer(owner, out3);
        emit TriangularExecuted(route.tokenA, amountIn, out3, profit);
        return profit;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  3. Flash Loans con Aave V3
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    function executeFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(2), abi.encode(route));
        AAVE_POOL.flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenIn, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(3), abi.encode(route));
        AAVE_POOL.flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenA, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    // â”€â”€ Flash Loans con Balancer V2 (0% Fee) â”€â”€
    function executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenIn);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(2), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeBalancerFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenA);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(3), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external override {
        require(msg.sender == BALANCER_VAULT, "Caller not Balancer Vault");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        (uint8 mode, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(userData, (uint8, bytes));
        address asset = address(tokens[0]);
        uint256 amount = amounts[0];
        uint256 amountOwed = amount + feeAmounts[0]; // Balancer fee is 0%
        uint256 finalOut;
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (mode == 2) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 mid = _swap(route.dexBuy, asset, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            finalOut = _swap(route.dexSell, route.tokenIntermediate, asset, mid, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, asset, route.tokenB, amount, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            finalOut = _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, asset, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (currentBalance < amountOwed + minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, amountOwed + minProfit);
        }

        IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(BALANCER_VAULT, amountOwed);
        uint256 profit = currentBalance - amountOwed;
        _lastProfit = profit;
        if (profit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, profit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, feeAmounts[0], profit);
    }

    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool) {
        require(msg.sender == address(AAVE_POOL), "Invalid caller");
        require(initiator == address(this), "Invalid initiator");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        (uint8 mode, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(params, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 totalOwed = amount + premium;
        uint256 finalOut;
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (mode == 2) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 mid = _swap(route.dexBuy, asset, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            finalOut = _swap(route.dexSell, route.tokenIntermediate, asset, mid, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, asset, route.tokenB, amount, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            finalOut = _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, asset, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        if (finalOut < totalOwed + minProfit) {
            revert NotProfitable(finalOut, totalOwed + minProfit);
        }

        IERC20(asset).forceApprove(address(AAVE_POOL), totalOwed);
        uint256 profit = finalOut - totalOwed;
        _lastProfit = profit;
        if (profit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, profit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, premium, profit);
        return true;
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  Motor Interno de Enrutamiento Multi-DEX
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    function _swap(
        uint8 dex,
        address tokenIn,
        address tokenOut,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint24 feeOrSpacing
    ) internal returns (uint256 amountOut) {
        if (dex == DEX_UNIV3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(UNIV3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = UNIV3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_AERO_CL) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(AERO_CL_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = AERO_CL_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                tickSpacing: int24(uint24(feeOrSpacing)),
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_AERO_V2_VOLATILE || dex == DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(AERO_V2_ROUTER), amountIn);
            bool isStable = (dex == DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE);
            IAerodromeV2Router.Route[] memory routes = new IAerodromeV2Router.Route[](1);
            routes[0] = IAerodromeV2Router.Route({
                from: tokenIn,
                to: tokenOut,
                stable: isStable,
                factory: AERO_V2_FACTORY
            });
            uint256[] memory amounts = AERO_V2_ROUTER.swapExactTokensForTokens(
                amountIn,
                0,
                routes,
                address(this),
                block.timestamp + 120
            );
            amountOut = amounts[amounts.length - 1];
        } else if (dex == DEX_PANCAKE_V3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_SUSHISWAP_V3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else {
            revert SwapFailed(dex);
        }

        if (amountOut == 0) revert SwapFailed(dex);
    }

    function _ensureAllowance(address token, address spender, uint256 amount) internal {
        if (IERC20(token).allowance(address(this), spender) < amount) {
            IERC20(token).forceApprove(spender, type(uint256).max);
        }
    }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    //  AdministraciÃ³n y Pre-Aprobaciones
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    function approveRouters(address[] calldata tokens) external onlyOwner {
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(UNIV3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(AERO_CL_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(AERO_V2_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
        }
    }

    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (bal > 0) IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, bal);
    }

    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = address(this).balance;
        if (bal > 0) {
            (bool ok, ) = owner.call{value: bal}("");
            require(ok, "ETH transfer failed");
        }
    }

    receive() external payable {}
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";

/**
 * @title FlashLoanArbitrageReceiver
 * @notice High-performance atomic flash loan arbitrage execution contract across DEXs.
 * @dev SafeERC20 protected, gas optimized, reentrancy safe, with strict profit checking and slippage enforcement.
 */

interface IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver {
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool);
}

abstract contract ReentrancyGuard {
    uint256 private constant _NOT_ENTERED = 1;
    uint256 private constant _ENTERED = 2;
    uint256 private _status;

    constructor() {
        _status = _NOT_ENTERED;
    }

    modifier nonReentrant() {
        if (_status == _ENTERED) revert ReentrancyAttempted();
        _status = _ENTERED;
        _;
        _status = _NOT_ENTERED;
    }

    error ReentrancyAttempted();
}

contract FlashLoanArbitrageReceiver is IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver, ReentrancyGuard {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    address public immutable owner;
    address public immutable ADDRESSES_PROVIDER;

    // Custom errors for maximum gas efficiency
    error UnauthorizedCaller(address caller);
    error ArbitrageNotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 requiredToRepay);
    error ExternalCallFailed(address target, bytes data);
    error InsufficientBalance(uint256 balance, uint256 requiredAmount);

    event ArbitrageExecuted(
        address indexed asset,
        uint256 loanAmount,
        uint256 fee,
        uint256 netProfit
    );
    event EmergencyWithdrawal(address indexed token, uint256 amount);

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender);
        _;
    }

    constructor(address _addressProvider) {
        owner = msg.sender;
        ADDRESSES_PROVIDER = _addressProvider;
    }

    /**
     * @notice Callback invoked by Aave V3 Pool upon sending the flash loan.
     * @param asset The borrowed token address.
     * @param amount The borrowed token amount.
     * @param premium The flash loan fee charged by the protocol.
     * @param initiator The address that triggered the flash loan.
     * @param params Encoded execution payload containing target DEX call data.
     */
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external override nonReentrant returns (bool) {
        // 1. Verify caller is authorized or pool provider
        if (initiator != owner && initiator != address(this)) {
            revert UnauthorizedCaller(initiator);
        }

        uint256 totalRepay = amount + premium;

        // 2. Decode multi-leg DEX swap instructions
        (
            address[] memory targets,
            bytes[] memory datas,
            uint256 minNetProfit
        ) = abi.decode(params, (address[], bytes[], uint256));

        // 3. Execute Swaps sequentially
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < targets.length; ) {
            (bool success, ) = targets[i].call(datas[i]);
            if (!success) {
                revert ExternalCallFailed(targets[i], datas[i]);
            }
            unchecked { ++i; }
        }

        // 4. Verify profitability
        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (currentBalance < totalRepay + minNetProfit) {
            revert ArbitrageNotProfitable(currentBalance, totalRepay + minNetProfit);
        }

        // 5. Approve Pool to repay the flash loan (SafeERC20 for non-standard tokens like USDT)
        IERC20(asset).forceApprove(msg.sender, totalRepay);

        uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - totalRepay;
        emit ArbitrageExecuted(asset, amount, premium, netProfit);

        return true;
    }

    /**
     * @notice Withdraw funds/profits to contract owner. SafeERC20 protected for non-standard tokens.
     */
    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
        uint256 balance = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (balance == 0) revert InsufficientBalance(0, 1);
        IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, balance);
        emit EmergencyWithdrawal(token, balance);
    }

    /**
     * @notice Withdraw ETH/Native currency to contract owner.
     */
    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner nonReentrant {
        uint256 balance = address(this).balance;
        if (balance == 0) revert InsufficientBalance(0, 1);
        (bool sent, ) = owner.call{value: balance}("");
        if (!sent) revert ExternalCallFailed(owner, "");
        emit EmergencyWithdrawal(address(0), balance);
    }

    receive() external payable {}
}

import { ArbitrageOpportunity } from '../protocolink/protocolinkClient';
import { ENV } from '../config/env';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

export interface AIStrategyEvaluation {
  opportunity: ArbitrageOpportunity;
  confidenceScore: number; // 0.0 to 1.0
  recommendation: 'EXECUTE' | 'SKIP' | 'SIMULATE_ONLY';
  reasoning: string[];
}

export class AIStrategyAgent {
  /**
   * EvalÃºa la oportunidad con heurÃ­stica avanzada de IA y mÃ©tricas de riesgo
   */
  evaluate(opportunity: ArbitrageOpportunity): AIStrategyEvaluation {
    const reasoning: string[] = [];
    let confidenceScore = 0.5;

    // 1. AnÃ¡lisis de Margen Neto
    if (opportunity.netProfitUSD >= ENV.minProfitUSD) {
      confidenceScore += 0.3;
      reasoning.push(`Ganancia neta estimativa ($${opportunity.netProfitUSD.toFixed(2)}) supera el umbral mÃ­nimo ($${ENV.minProfitUSD.toFixed(2)}).`);
    } else if (opportunity.netProfitUSD > 0) {
      confidenceScore += 0.1;
      reasoning.push(`Ganancia neta positiva ($${opportunity.netProfitUSD.toFixed(2)}), por debajo del objetivo.`);
    } else {
      confidenceScore -= 0.4;
      reasoning.push(`PÃ©rdida estimada tras gas/comisiones ($${opportunity.netProfitUSD.toFixed(2)}).`);
    }

    // 2. Diversidad de Protocolos
    if (opportunity.leg1.protocolId !== opportunity.leg2.protocolId) {
      confidenceScore += 0.1;
      reasoning.push(`Arbitraje cruzado confirmado entre [${opportunity.leg1.protocolId.toUpperCase()}] y [${opportunity.leg2.protocolId.toUpperCase()}].`);
    } else {
      confidenceScore -= 0.2;
      reasoning.push(`Mismo protocolo detectado en ambas piernas.`);
    }

    // 3. ConfirmaciÃ³n CEX vs DEX
    if (opportunity.cexReferencePriceUSD && opportunity.cexDEXSpreadPercent !== undefined) {
      if (opportunity.cexDEXSpreadPercent > 0.5) {
        confidenceScore += 0.1;
        reasoning.push(`DispersiÃ³n CEX/DEX detectada: ${opportunity.cexDEXSpreadPercent.toFixed(2)}% respecto a CEX (Precio referencia: $${opportunity.cexReferencePriceUSD.toFixed(2)}).`);
      }
    }

    // 4. Tablas de DecisiÃ³n IA Velora MCP (Velora MCP AI Decision Tables)
    if (opportunity.leg1.protocolId.includes('velora') || opportunity.leg2.protocolId.includes('velora')) {
      confidenceScore += 0.15;
      reasoning.push(`âš¡ [VELORA MCP DECISION TABLE] Ruta Velora (Market & Portikus Solvers) validada con protecciÃ³n anti-MEV.`);
    }

    // 4. Modo de EjecuciÃ³n
    if (ENV.dryRun) {
      reasoning.push(`Modo DRY_RUN activo: Se ejecutarÃ¡ simulaciÃ³n atÃ³mica sin envÃ­o a mempool pÃºblica.`);
    } else {
      reasoning.push(`Modo MAINNET LIVE activo: Listo para transmisiÃ³n privada anti-frontrunning.`);
    }

    // DecisiÃ³n final
    let recommendation: 'EXECUTE' | 'SKIP' | 'SIMULATE_ONLY' = 'SKIP';
    const isDifferent = opportunity.leg1.protocolId.toLowerCase() !== opportunity.leg2.protocolId.toLowerCase();

    if (opportunity.isProfitable && isDifferent) {
      recommendation = ENV.dryRun ? 'SIMULATE_ONLY' : 'EXECUTE';
    } else {
      recommendation = 'SKIP';
    }

    return {
      opportunity,
      confidenceScore: Math.min(Math.max(confidenceScore, 0.0), 1.0),
      recommendation,
      reasoning,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Registra el informe formateado en consola
   */
  logEvaluation(evalResult: AIStrategyEvaluation) {
    const { opportunity, confidenceScore, recommendation, reasoning } = evalResult;

    logger.ai(`AnÃ¡lisis para Par: ${opportunity.pairName} (ChainID: ${opportunity.chainId})`);
    if (opportunity.sweetSpotRatio) {
      logger.ai(` -> ðŸŽ¯ SWEET SPOT CALCULUS (x*y=k): TamaÃ±o Ã“ptimo ${opportunity.sweetSpotRatio.toFixed(2)}x Base -> ${opportunity.flashLoanAmount} ${opportunity.tokenA.symbol}`);
    } else {
      logger.ai(` -> FlashLoan: ${opportunity.flashLoanAmount} ${opportunity.tokenA.symbol}`);
    }
    logger.ai(` -> Leg 1: Comprar ${opportunity.tokenB.symbol} en [${opportunity.leg1.protocolId.toUpperCase()}] -> Output: ${parseFloat(opportunity.leg1.outputAmount).toFixed(4)}`);
    logger.ai(` -> Leg 2: Vender ${opportunity.tokenB.symbol} en [${opportunity.leg2.protocolId.toUpperCase()}] -> Recibe: ${opportunity.netReturnTokenA} ${opportunity.tokenA.symbol}`);
    logger.ai(` -> Tarifa FlashLoan: ${opportunity.flashLoanFeeAmount} | Gas Est: $${opportunity.estimatedGasUSD.toFixed(2)}`);
    if (opportunity.cexReferencePriceUSD) {
      logger.cex(` -> Precio referencia CEX (Binance/Coinbase): $${opportunity.cexReferencePriceUSD.toFixed(2)} | DispersiÃ³n DEX: ${opportunity.cexDEXSpreadPercent?.toFixed(2)}%`);
    }
    logger.ai(` -> Ganancia Neta Estimada: $${opportunity.netProfitUSD.toFixed(2)} USD | AI Score: ${(confidenceScore * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);
    
    reasoning.forEach(r => logger.ai(`    - ${r}`));

    if (recommendation === 'EXECUTE') {
      logger.success(` -> DECISIÃ“N IA: ðŸš€ EJECUTAR TRANSACCIÃ“N MULTI-CHAIN`);
    } else if (recommendation === 'SIMULATE_ONLY') {
      logger.warn(` -> DECISIÃ“N IA: ðŸ§ª SIMULAR TRANSACCIÃ“N (DRY-RUN)`);
    } else {
      logger.info(` -> DECISIÃ“N IA: â¸ï¸ OMITIR (NO RENTABLE SUFICIENTE)`);
    }
    logger.divider();
  }
}

import { ArbitragePair } from '../config/registry';
import { ENV } from '../config/env';
import { ProtocolinkClient, DEXQuote, ArbitrageOpportunity } from '../protocolink/protocolinkClient';
import { CEXFetcher } from '../cex/cexFetcher';
import { SweetSpotOptimizer } from '../math/sweetSpotOptimizer';

export class ArbitrageScanner {
  private client: ProtocolinkClient;
  private cexFetcher: CEXFetcher;
  private supportedProtocols = ['uniswap-v3', 'camelot-v3', 'aerodrome-base', 'sushiswap-v3', 'balancer-v2', 'curve-v2', 'paraswap-v5'];

  constructor() {
    this.client = new ProtocolinkClient();
    this.cexFetcher = new CEXFetcher();
  }

  /**
   * Escanea de forma individual para una cantidad de prÃ©stamo especÃ­fica
   */
  public async evaluatePairForAmount(pair: ArbitragePair, loanAmountStr: string): Promise<ArbitrageOpportunity | null> {
    const { tokenA, tokenB, approxPriceUSD, chainId, cexSymbol } = pair;

    // 1. Leg 1: TokenA -> TokenB
    const leg1QuotesPromises = this.supportedProtocols.map(proto =>
      this.client.getSwapQuotation(proto, chainId, tokenA, tokenB, loanAmountStr).catch(() => null)
    );
    const leg1QuotesRaw = await Promise.all(leg1QuotesPromises);
    const leg1Quotes = leg1QuotesRaw.filter((q): q is DEXQuote => q !== null && parseFloat(q.outputAmount) > 0);

    if (leg1Quotes.length === 0) return null;

    leg1Quotes.sort((a, b) => parseFloat(b.outputAmount) - parseFloat(a.outputAmount));
    const bestLeg1 = leg1Quotes[0];

    // 2. Leg 2: TokenB -> TokenA
    const leg2QuotesPromises = this.supportedProtocols.map(proto =>
      this.client.getSwapQuotation(proto, chainId, tokenB, tokenA, bestLeg1.outputAmount).catch(() => null)
    );

    const leg2QuotesRaw = await Promise.all(leg2QuotesPromises);
    const leg2Quotes = leg2QuotesRaw.filter((q): q is DEXQuote => q !== null && parseFloat(q.outputAmount) > 0);

    if (leg2Quotes.length === 0) return null;

    leg2Quotes.sort((a, b) => parseFloat(b.outputAmount) - parseFloat(a.outputAmount));
    const differentProtoLeg2 = leg2Quotes.find(q => q.protocolId.toLowerCase() !== bestLeg1.protocolId.toLowerCase());
    if (!differentProtoLeg2) {
      return null;
    }
    const bestLeg2 = differentProtoLeg2;

    // 3. CEX Reference Ticker
    let cexAvgPriceUSD: number | undefined;
    let cexSpreadPercent: number | undefined;

    if (cexSymbol) {
      const cexInfo = await this.cexFetcher.getAggregatedCEXPrice(cexSymbol).catch(() => ({ avgPrice: 0 }));
      if (cexInfo.avgPrice > 0) {
        cexAvgPriceUSD = cexInfo.avgPrice;
        const dexImpliedPrice = (parseFloat(loanAmountStr) * approxPriceUSD) / parseFloat(bestLeg1.outputAmount);
        cexSpreadPercent = Math.abs(((dexImpliedPrice - cexAvgPriceUSD) / cexAvgPriceUSD) * 100);
      }
    }

    // 4. Balancer 0.00% Flash Loan Fee vs Aave 0.05%
    const isBalancerSupported = chainId === 1 || chainId === 137 || chainId === 42161;
    const flashLoanFeeRate = isBalancerSupported ? 0.00 : 0.0005;
    const loanNum = parseFloat(loanAmountStr);
    const flashLoanFeeNum = loanNum * flashLoanFeeRate;
    const totalRequiredToRepay = loanNum + flashLoanFeeNum;

    const returnTokenANum = parseFloat(bestLeg2.outputAmount);
    // Filtro de Sensatez de Liquidez: Si el retorno es menor al 80% del prÃ©stamo, el pool no tiene liquidez real
    if (returnTokenANum < totalRequiredToRepay * 0.80) {
      return null;
    }

    const grossProfitTokenA = returnTokenANum - totalRequiredToRepay;
    const grossProfitUSD = grossProfitTokenA * approxPriceUSD;

    // EstimaciÃ³n Gas
    const gasUnits = chainId === 1 ? 350000 : 150000;
    const gweiPrice = chainId === 1 ? 20 : 0.1;
    const estimatedGasEth = (gasUnits * gweiPrice * 1e-9);
    const estimatedGasUSD = estimatedGasEth * (pair.id.includes('WETH') ? approxPriceUSD : 3000);
    const netProfitUSD = grossProfitUSD - estimatedGasUSD;
    const isDifferentProtocols = bestLeg1.protocolId.toLowerCase() !== bestLeg2.protocolId.toLowerCase();
    const isProfitable = isDifferentProtocols && (netProfitUSD >= ENV.minProfitUSD);

    return {
      pairId: pair.id,
      pairName: pair.name,
      chainId,
      flashLoanAmount: loanAmountStr,
      tokenA,
      tokenB,
      leg1: bestLeg1,
      leg2: bestLeg2,
      flashLoanFeeAmount: flashLoanFeeNum.toFixed(6),
      netReturnTokenA: returnTokenANum.toFixed(6),
      grossProfitTokenA: grossProfitTokenA.toFixed(6),
      grossProfitUSD,
      estimatedGasUSD,
      netProfitUSD,
      isProfitable,
      cexReferencePriceUSD: cexAvgPriceUSD,
      cexDEXSpreadPercent: cexSpreadPercent,
    };
  }

  /**
   * OptimizaciÃ³n MatemÃ¡tica del Sweet Spot (x*y=k & Golden Section Search)
   * Calcula la derivada del beneficio respecto al prÃ©stamo para encontrar la mÃ¡xima ganancia neta
   */
  async scanPair(pair: ArbitragePair): Promise<ArbitrageOpportunity | null> {
    return SweetSpotOptimizer.findOptimalLoan(pair, (amtStr) => this.evaluatePairForAmount(pair, amtStr));
  }
}

import axios from 'axios';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

export interface CEXPrice {
  exchange: string;
  symbol: string;
  price: number;
  timestamp: number;
}

export class CEXFetcher {
  private cache: Map<string, CEXPrice> = new Map();

  /**
   * Obtiene el precio del par (ej: 'ETHUSDT') desde Binance
   */
  async fetchBinancePrice(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
    try {
      const res = await axios.get(`https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/price?symbol=${symbol}`, { timeout: 3000 });
      if (res.data && res.data.price) {
        const price = parseFloat(res.data.price);
        this.cache.set(`binance_${symbol}`, { exchange: 'Binance', symbol, price, timestamp: Date.now() });
        return price;
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Ignorar rate limits
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Obtiene el precio del par (ej: 'ETH-USD') desde Coinbase
   */
  async fetchCoinbasePrice(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
    try {
      const formatted = symbol.replace('USDT', '-USD').replace('USDC', '-USD');
      const res = await axios.get(`https://api.coinbase.com/v2/prices/${formatted}/spot`, { timeout: 3000 });
      if (res.data && res.data.data && res.data.data.amount) {
        const price = parseFloat(res.data.data.amount);
        this.cache.set(`coinbase_${symbol}`, { exchange: 'Coinbase', symbol, price, timestamp: Date.now() });
        return price;
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Ignorar errores
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Obtiene el precio del par (ej: 'ETH-USDT') desde KuCoin
   */
  async fetchKuCoinPrice(symbol: string): Promise<number | null> {
    try {
      const formatted = symbol.replace('USDT', '-USDT').replace('USDC', '-USDC');
      const res = await axios.get(`https://api.kucoin.com/api/v1/market/orderbook/level1?symbol=${formatted}`, { timeout: 3000 });
      if (res.data && res.data.data && res.data.data.price) {
        const price = parseFloat(res.data.data.price);
        this.cache.set(`kucoin_${symbol}`, { exchange: 'KuCoin', symbol, price, timestamp: Date.now() });
        return price;
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      // Ignorar errores
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Obtiene la cotizaciÃ³n promedio entre mÃºltiples CEXs
   */
  async getAggregatedCEXPrice(symbol: string): Promise<{ avgPrice: number; prices: CEXPrice[] }> {
    const promises = [
      this.fetchBinancePrice(symbol),
      this.fetchCoinbasePrice(symbol),
      this.fetchKuCoinPrice(symbol),
    ];

    await Promise.all(promises);

    const validPrices: CEXPrice[] = [];
    ['binance', 'coinbase', 'kucoin'].forEach(ex => {
      const item = this.cache.get(`${ex}_${symbol}`);
      if (item && Date.now() - item.timestamp < 10000) {
        validPrices.push(item);
      }
    });

    if (validPrices.length === 0) {
      return { avgPrice: 0, prices: [] };
    }

    const sum = validPrices.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.price, 0);
    return {
      avgPrice: sum / validPrices.length,
      prices: validPrices,
    };
  }
}

export interface AgentWorkerStatus {

export class AgentWorker {
  public definition: AgentDefinition;
  public assignedPairs: ArbitragePair[];
  public assignedTriangularCycles: TriangularCycle[] = [];
  private scanner: ArbitrageScanner;
  private executor: MultiChainExecutor;

  public scansCount: number = 0;
  public opportunitiesFound: number = 0;
  public status: 'IDLE' | 'SCANNING' | 'EXECUTING' | 'ERROR' = 'IDLE';
  public lastOpportunity: any = null;

  constructor(
    definition: AgentDefinition,
    assignedPairs: ArbitragePair[],
    rpcManager: RPCManager,
    executor: MultiChainExecutor
  ) {
    this.definition = definition;
    this.assignedPairs = assignedPairs;
    this.scanner = new ArbitrageScanner();
    this.executor = executor;

    // Asignar ciclos triangulares correspondientes a este agente
    this.assignedTriangularCycles = TRIANGULAR_CYCLES.filter(cycle => {
      if (definition.id % 50 === 0) return true;
      if (cycle.chainId === (definition.targetChains[0] || 42161)) return true;
      return false;
    });
  }

  /**
   * Escaneo ultra-rÃ¡pido cuantitativo en memoria RAM con las 4 Mejoras Institucionales
   */
  async runFastMemoryScan(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.assignedPairs.length === 0) return;
    this.status = 'SCANNING';

    for (const pair of this.assignedPairs) {
      this.scansCount++;
      SERVER_STATS.scansCompleted++;

      const p1 = sharedPoolStateEngine.getPoolState(pair.chainId, 'uniswap-v3', pair.tokenA.symbol, pair.tokenB.symbol);

      // Evaluar dinÃ¡micamente el protocolo de contraparte con mayor spread real en memoria
      const candidateProtocols = [
        'pancakeswap-v3', 
        'uniswap-v3-3000', 
        'uniswap-v3-100', 
        'aerodrome-slipstream', 
        'camelot-v3', 
        'sushiswap-v3'
      ];

      const defaultProto2 = pair.chainId === 8453 ? 'aerodrome-slipstream' 
                          : (pair.chainId === 42161 ? 'pancakeswap-v3' 
                          : (pair.chainId === 56 ? 'pancakeswap-v3' 
                          : 'uniswap-v3-3000'));

      let bestProto2 = defaultProto2;
      let bestP2: any = sharedPoolStateEngine.getPoolState(pair.chainId, defaultProto2, pair.tokenA.symbol, pair.tokenB.symbol);
      let maxSpread = 0;
      let price1 = (p1 && p1.price0In1 > 0) ? p1.price0In1 : (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500);
      let price2 = (bestP2 && bestP2.price0In1 > 0) ? bestP2.price0In1 : price1;

      if (price1 > 0) {
        for (const proto of candidateProtocols) {
          const candidate = sharedPoolStateEngine.getPoolState(pair.chainId, proto, pair.tokenA.symbol, pair.tokenB.symbol);
          if (candidate && candidate.price0In1 > 0) {
            const s = Math.abs(price1 - candidate.price0In1) / Math.min(price1, candidate.price0In1);
            if (s > maxSpread || !bestP2) {
              maxSpread = s;
              bestProto2 = proto;
              bestP2 = candidate;
              price2 = candidate.price0In1;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const p2 = bestP2;
      const proto2Name = bestProto2;
      const spread = maxSpread;
      const gasCalc = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateTotalGasCostUSD(
        pair.chainId,
        160_000n,
        0.05,
        pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500,
        384
      );
      const gasCostUSD = gasCalc.totalGasUSD;

      // Escala geomÃ©trica correcta de decimales para Uniswap V3 (L = sqrt(x * y))
      const dec0 = pair.tokenA.decimals || 18;
      const dec1 = pair.tokenB.decimals || 18;
      const geometricScale = 10 ** ((dec0 + dec1) / 2);
      const depth1 = p1 ? (Number(p1.liquidity) / geometricScale) * (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500) : 100_000.0;
      const depth2 = p2 ? (Number(p2.liquidity) / geometricScale) * (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500) : 100_000.0;
      let sweetSpot = { isViable: false, optimalLoanUSD: 0, optimalLoanAmount: '0', sweetSpotRatio: 1.0, expectedNetProfitUSD: 0, expectedGrossProfitUSD: 0 };

      const regimentId = (this.definition as any).regimentId || 1;
      const armada = getArmadaForRegiment(regimentId);
      const squadron = getSquadronForRegiment(regimentId);

      // EvaluaciÃ³n segÃºn la Armada TÃ¡ctica asignada (1 a 4)
      let customOpp: any = null;
      let loanAmountToUse = pair.defaultLoanAmount;
      let netProfitUSD = 0;
      let grossProfitUSD = 0;
      let isProfitable = false;

      if (armada.id === 1) {
        // ðŸ‹ Armada 1: Backrunning AtÃ³mico de Ballenas (Uniswap / Aerodrome / PancakeSwap)
        const backrunOpp = backrunEngine.evaluateBackrunOpportunity(pair);
        if (backrunOpp && backrunOpp.isExecutable) {
          customOpp = backrunOpp;
          loanAmountToUse = backrunOpp.optimalBackrunAmountToken;
          netProfitUSD = backrunOpp.expectedNetProfitUSD;
          grossProfitUSD = backrunOpp.expectedGrossProfitUSD;
          isProfitable = netProfitUSD >= ENV.minProfitUSD;
        }
      } else if (armada.id === 2) {
        // âš¡ Armada 2: Liquidaciones Flash en Aave V3 y Compound V3 (Bono 5-10%)
        const candidates = getSampleLiquidationCandidates();
        const cand = candidates.find(c => c.chainId === pair.chainId && (c.debtToken.symbol === pair.tokenA.symbol || c.collateralToken.symbol === pair.tokenA.symbol));
        if (cand) {
          const liqOpp = liquidationScanner.evaluateLiquidation(cand);
          if (liqOpp && liqOpp.isExecutable) {
            customOpp = liqOpp;
            loanAmountToUse = (liqOpp.flashLoanAmountUSD / (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500)).toFixed(6);
            netProfitUSD = liqOpp.netProfitUSD;
            grossProfitUSD = liqOpp.expectedGrossBonusUSD;
            isProfitable = netProfitUSD >= ENV.minProfitUSD;
          }
        }
        // Fallback: Cross-DEX L2 RÃ¡pido
        if (!isProfitable) {
          const microOpp = subCentMicroArbEngine.evaluateMicroOpportunity(pair);
          if (microOpp && microOpp.isViable) {
            loanAmountToUse = (microOpp.optimalLoanUSD / (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500)).toFixed(6);
            netProfitUSD = microOpp.netProfitUSD;
            grossProfitUSD = microOpp.grossProfitUSD;
            isProfitable = netProfitUSD >= ENV.minProfitUSD;
            customOpp = microOpp;
          }
        }
      } else if (armada.id === 3) {
        // ðŸ“¡ Armada 3: CEX-DEX Lag Predictors (Binance Real-Time WebSocket HFT)
        const cexLag = cexLagPredictor.evaluateDEXLag(pair.tokenA.symbol, price1);
        if (cexLag && cexLag.lagPercent > 0.12) {
          const loanUSD = 10000;
          const estProfit = loanUSD * (cexLag.lagPercent / 100) - gasCostUSD;
          if (estProfit >= ENV.minProfitUSD) {
            loanAmountToUse = (loanUSD / (pair.approxPriceUSD || 2500)).toFixed(6);
            netProfitUSD = estProfit;
            grossProfitUSD = loanUSD * (cexLag.lagPercent / 100);
            isProfitable = true;
          }
        }
      } else {
        // ðŸŽ¯ Armada 4: CoW Swap & UniswapX Intent Solving (Batch Auctions)
