import { ENV } from './config/env';
import { MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS, SWARM_AGENTS } from './config/registry';
import { SUPPORTED_CHAINS } from './config/chains';
import { RPCManager } from './providers/rpcManager';
import { SwarmManager } from './cluster/swarmManager';
import { startServer, setSwarmManager } from './server';
import { logger } from './utils/logger';
import { telegramNotifier } from './notifications/telegramNotifier';
import { loadPersistedStats } from './utils/statsStore';
import { Permit2ApprovalManager } from './executor/permit2ApprovalManager';

async function main() {
  // Cargar estadÃ­sticas histÃ³ricas desde disco
  loadPersistedStats();

  logger.divider();
  logger.info('ðŸš€ INICIANDO CLUSTER DE 10 AGENTES SWARM DE ARBITRAJE FLASH LOAN V2 (OPTIMIZADO)');
  logger.info(`   Agentes Concurrentes: ${SWARM_AGENTS.length} Agentes Especializados`);
  logger.info(`   Pares de Liquidez Totales: ${MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS.length} Pares Multi-Cadena`);
  logger.info(`   Modo DRY_RUN: ${ENV.dryRun ? 'ACTIVADO (SimulaciÃ³n sin riesgo)' : 'DESACTIVADO (MODO LIVE MAINNET REAL)'}`);
  logger.info(`   Ganancia MÃ­nima Objetivo: $${ENV.minProfitUSD.toFixed(2)} USD`);
  logger.info(`   Optimizaciones MEV: Sweet Spot Loan Search | Balancer 0% Fee | WSS Sub-Segundo | Flashbots Relays`);
  logger.divider();

  // 1. Inicializar Gestor de RPCs y cargar Chainlist.org
  const rpcManager = new RPCManager();
  await rpcManager.loadChainlistRPCs();

  // 2. Garantizar autorizaciones de Uniswap Permit2 y preparar Spot Executor en Base
  const permit2Manager = new Permit2ApprovalManager(rpcManager);
  await permit2Manager.ensureAllPermit2Approvals().catch((e) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Permit2 approvals: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { spotExecutor } = require('./executor/spotExecutor');
  await spotExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Spot Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { arbitrumExecutor } = require('./executor/arbitrumExecutor');
  await arbitrumExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Arbitrum Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { lowFeeExecutor } = require('./executor/lowFeeExecutor');
  await lowFeeExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Low-Fee Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  // Benchmark preliminar para TODAS las cadenas (Ethereum, Arbitrum, Optimism, Polygon, Base, BSC, Avalanche)
  logger.info('Iniciando benchmark de latencia RPC para todas las cadenas soportadas...');
  for (const chainIdStr of Object.keys(SUPPORTED_CHAINS)) {
    const chainId = parseInt(chainIdStr, 10);
    await rpcManager.benchmarkAndSelectBestRPC(chainId).catch(() => {});
    rpcManager.subscribeNewHeads(chainId, (blockNumber) => {
      // Disparo inmediato por WebSocket en milisegundo 0
    });
  }

  // 2. Inicializar Swarm Cluster de 10 Agentes
  const swarmManager = new SwarmManager(rpcManager);
  setSwarmManager(swarmManager);

  // 3. Iniciar Servidor de API HTTP
  startServer();

  // 4. Notificar a Telegram
  await telegramNotifier.notifyStartup(MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS.length, SWARM_AGENTS.length).catch(() => {});

  // 5. Arrancar ciclo del Cluster
  await swarmManager.startSwarmLoop();
}

main().catch((err) => {
  logger.error(`Fallo crÃ­tico al iniciar el cluster de agentes: ${err.message}`);
  process.exit(1);
});

import express from 'express';

const app = express();

app.use(express.urlencoded({ limit: '50mb', extended: true }));

export let globalSwarmManager: SwarmManager | null = null;
export const LIVE_PAIR_RESULTS: Record<string, any> = {};

export const RECENT_SCAN_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const SPOT_BASE_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const FLASH_LOAN_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const LOW_FEE_LOGS: string[] = [];

export function addScanLog(msg: string, category?: string) {
  const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  const formatted = `[${timestamp}] ${msg}`;
  
  RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
  if (RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.length > 50) RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.pop();

  if (category === 'LOW_FEE' || msg.includes('LOW-FEE') || msg.includes('0.05%') || msg.includes('0.01%')) {
    LOW_FEE_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (LOW_FEE_LOGS.length > 50) LOW_FEE_LOGS.pop();
  } else if (category === 'SPOT_BASE' || msg.includes('SPOT') || msg.includes('TRIANGULAR') || msg.includes('Base') || msg.includes('8453')) {
    SPOT_BASE_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (SPOT_BASE_LOGS.length > 50) SPOT_BASE_LOGS.pop();
  } else {
    FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.length > 50) FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.pop();
  }
}

export function setSwarmManager(manager: SwarmManager) {
  globalSwarmManager = manager;
  streamProcessor.setSwarmManager(manager);
}

export function addSpotOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'SPOT_BASE';
  opp.chainId = 8453;
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

export function addLowFeeOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'LOW_FEE';
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName && o.chainId === opp.chainId);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

export function addFlashOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'FLASH_LOAN';
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName && o.chainId === opp.chainId);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

function updateMergedOpportunities() {
  SERVER_STATS.lastOpportunities = [
    ...SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities,
    ...SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities,
    ...SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities
  ];
}

    if (payload && payload.eventType) {
      streamProcessor.processStreamEvent(payload);
      addScanLog(`âš¡ [QUICKNODE STREAM] Evento ${payload.eventType} recibido para ${payload.pairName || 'Pool'}`, 'FLASH_LOAN');
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, processed: true });
    }
    return res.status(200).json({ success: true, status: 'CONNECTED' });
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.warn(`Error al procesar payload de QuickNode Stream: ${err.message}`);
    return res.status(200).json({ success: true, status: 'CONNECTED' });
  }
});

export function startServer(initialPort: number = ENV.port) {
  loadPersistedStats();
  cexLagPredictor.startMonitoring(2000).catch(() => {});
  binanceWebSocketFeed.start();
  const server = app.listen(initialPort, () => {
    logger.info(`ðŸŒ Servidor de monitoreo V2 ejecutÃ¡ndose en http://localhost:${initialPort}`);
    logger.info(`   -> Dashboard Web: http://localhost:${initialPort}`);
    logger.info(`   -> Endpoint Webhook QuickNode Streams: http://localhost:${initialPort}/api/mempool-stream`);
  });

  server.on('error', (err: any) => {
    if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
      const nextPort = initialPort + 1;
      logger.warn(`Puerto ${initialPort} ocupado. Intentando en http://localhost:${nextPort}...`);
      startServer(nextPort);
    } else {
      logger.error(`Error en servidor API: ${err.message}`);
    }
  });
}

if (require.main === module) {
  startServer();
}

import { ethers } from "hardhat";
import dotenv from "dotenv";
dotenv.config();

const WETH_BASE_ADDRESS = "0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006";
const ERC20_ABI = [
  "function allowance(address owner, address spender) external view returns (uint256)",
  "function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool)",
  "function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256)"
];

async function main() {
  const contractAddress = process.env.ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE;
  if (!contractAddress) {
    throw new Error("No ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE found in .env");
  }

  const [signer] = await ethers.getSigners();
  console.log("ðŸ‘¤ Wallet:", signer.address);
  console.log("ðŸ“ Contrato Arbitraje:", contractAddress);

  const weth = new ethers.Contract(WETH_BASE_ADDRESS, ERC20_ABI, signer);
  const bal = await weth.balanceOf(signer.address);
  console.log("ðŸ’° Saldo WETH:", ethers.formatEther(bal), "WETH");

  const allowance = await weth.allowance(signer.address, contractAddress);
  console.log("ðŸ” Allowance actual:", ethers.formatEther(allowance), "WETH");

  if (allowance < ethers.parseEther("1.0")) {
    console.log("âš¡ Aprobando WETH para el contrato de arbitraje...");
    const tx = await weth.approve(contractAddress, ethers.MaxUint256);
    await tx.wait(1);
    console.log("âœ… WETH aprobado exitosamente para el contrato!");
  } else {
    console.log("âœ… WETH ya cuenta con aprobaciÃ³n suficiente.");
  }
}

main().catch(console.error);

  username: process.env.VPS_USER || 'root',

};

const conn = new Client();

conn.on('ready', () => {
  const cmd = `
pm2 logs arbitrage-cluster --lines 30 --nostream
curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/status
`;

  conn.exec(cmd, (err, stream) => {
    if (err) {
      console.error('Error:', err);
      conn.end();
      return;
    }

    stream.on('data', (d) => process.stdout.write(d.toString()));
    stream.on('close', (code) => {
      conn.end();
      process.exit(0);
    });
  });
});

conn.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('Error SSH:', err.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

FILE: C:\Users\Jose\Desktop\PELI_TRIAUDIO\flashloan-arbitrage-agent-v2\scripts\decode_revert.js

const { ethers } = require('ethers');
require('dotenv').config();

const ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE = process.env.ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE || '0xAD04bf95c85FF99aF80d885132B52A619C5F52BD';
const RPC_URL = 'https://developer-access-mainnet.base.org';

const iface = new ethers.Interface([
  'function executeSpotTriangular((address tokenA, address tokenB, address tokenC, uint8 dex1, uint8 dex2, uint8 dex3, uint24 feeOrSpacing1, uint24 feeOrSpacing2, uint24 feeOrSpacing3, uint256 minProfit) route, uint256 amountIn) external',
  'error NotOwner()',
  'error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required)',
  'error Reentrancy()',
  'error SwapFailed(uint8 leg)'
]);

const WETH = '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006';
const USDC = '0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913';
const CBBTC = '0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf';

async function testExactMath() {
  const provider = new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(RPC_URL, 8453);
  const formattedKey = process.env.PRIVATE_KEY.startsWith('0x') ? process.env.PRIVATE_KEY : ('0x' + process.env.PRIVATE_KEY);
  const wallet = new ethers.Wallet(formattedKey, provider);

  const tradeAmountIn = ethers.parseEther('0.014'); // 0.014 WETH ($35 USD)

  const routeStruct = {
    tokenA: WETH,
    tokenB: CBBTC,
    tokenC: USDC,
    dex1: 0, // UniV3
    dex2: 0, // UniV3
    dex3: 0, // UniV3
    feeOrSpacing1: 100, // 0.01%
    feeOrSpacing2: 500, // 0.05%
    feeOrSpacing3: 500, // 0.05%
    minProfit: 0n
  };

  const calldata = iface.encodeFunctionData('executeSpotTriangular', [routeStruct, tradeAmountIn]);

  try {
    const res = await provider.call({
      to: ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE,
      from: wallet.address,
      data: calldata
    });
    console.log('âœ… Ã‰xito! Res:', res);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.data) {
      try {
        const decoded = iface.parseError(err.data);
        console.log('ðŸ” Error Decodificado On-Chain:', decoded.name, decoded.args);
        if (decoded.name === 'NotProfitable') {
          const received = decoded.args[0];
          const required = decoded.args[1];
          const receivedEth = ethers.formatEther(received);
          const requiredEth = ethers.formatEther(required);
          const lossWei = required - received;
          const lossEth = ethers.formatEther(lossWei);
          const lossUSD = parseFloat(lossEth) * 2475;
          console.log(`\n================ RESULTADO MATEMÃTICO REAL ================`);
          console.log(`Entrada ($35 USD):     ${requiredEth} WETH ($${(parseFloat(requiredEth) * 2475).toFixed(2)} USD)`);
          console.log(`Salida Real del Pool: ${receivedEth} WETH ($${(parseFloat(receivedEth) * 2475).toFixed(2)} USD)`);
          console.log(`PÃ©rdida por Comisiones: -${lossEth} WETH (-$${lossUSD.toFixed(4)} USD)`);
          console.log(`Tasa de Retorno:       ${((parseFloat(receivedEth) / parseFloat(requiredEth)) * 100).toFixed(3)}%`);
          console.log(`============================================================`);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.log('Error al decodificar err.data:', e.message, err.data);
      }
    } else {
      console.log('Err sin data:', err.message);
    }
  }
}

testExactMath().catch(console.error);

import { ethers } from "hardhat";

import path from "path";

async function main() {
  console.log("ðŸš€ Iniciando despliegue de BaseMultiDexArbitrage en Base Mainnet (ChainID 8453)...");

  const [deployer] = await ethers.getSigners();
  const balance = await ethers.provider.getBalance(deployer.address);
  console.log("ðŸ‘¤ Deployer:", deployer.address);
  console.log("ðŸ’° Saldo ETH Nativo para Gas:", ethers.formatEther(balance), "ETH");

  if (balance === 0n) {
    throw new Error("âŒ Saldo insuficiente de ETH nativo en Base para pagar el gas de despliegue.");
  }

  // 1. Desplegar Contrato
  console.log("ðŸ“¦ Compilando y transmitiendo transacciÃ³n de despliegue...");
  const ArbitrageFactory = await ethers.getContractFactory("BaseMultiDexArbitrage");
  const contract = await ArbitrageFactory.deploy();
  await contract.waitForDeployment();

  const contractAddress = await contract.getAddress();
  console.log("ðŸŽ‰ Â¡CONTRATO DESPLEGADO EXITOSAMENTE!");
  console.log("ðŸ“ DirecciÃ³n del Contrato:", contractAddress);
  console.log("ðŸ”— Ver en BaseScan: https://basescan.org/address/" + contractAddress);

  // 2. Pre-aprobar los tokens principales para ahorrar gas en las ejecuciones
  const TOKENS_TO_APPROVE = [
    "0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006", // WETH
    "0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913", // USDC
    "0xd9aAEc86B65D86f6A7B5B1b0c42FFA531710b6CA", // USDbC
    "0x940181a94A35A4569E4529A3CDfB74e38FD98631", // AERO
    "0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf", // cbBTC
    "0x4ed4E862860beD51a9570b96d89aF5E1B0Efefed", // DEGEN
    "0x0b3e328455c4059EEb9e3f84b5543F74E24e7E1b", // VIRTUAL
    "0x532f27101965dd16442E59d40670FaF5eBB142E4", // BRETT
    "0xAC1bD2447a10150D01025454429261eD16f57b66", // TOSHI
  ];

  console.log("âš¡ Ejecutando approveRouters() para los 9 tokens clave...");
  const txApprove = await contract.approveRouters(TOKENS_TO_APPROVE);
  await txApprove.wait(1);
  console.log("âœ… Routers Uniswap V3 y Aerodrome Slipstream pre-aprobados con Ã©xito.");

  // 3. Guardar direcciÃ³n en el archivo .env
  const envPath = path.join(process.cwd(), ".env");
  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
    let envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
    if (envContent.includes("ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=")) {
      envContent = envContent.replace(/ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=.*/, `ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=${contractAddress}`);
    } else {
      envContent += `\nARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=${contractAddress}\n`;
    }
    fs.writeFileSync(envPath, envContent, "utf8");
    console.log("ðŸ’¾ DirecciÃ³n guardada en .env como ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE");
  }
}

import { ethers } from "hardhat";
import * as dotenv from "dotenv";
import * as fs from "fs";
import * as path from "path";

dotenv.config();

const TOKENS_TO_APPROVE = [
  "0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006", // WETH
  "0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913", // USDC
  "0xd9aAEc86B65D86f6A7B5B1b0c42FFA531710b6CA", // USDbC
  "0x940181a94A35A4569E4529A3CDfB74e38FD98631", // AERO
  "0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf", // cbBTC
  "0x4ed4E862860beD51a9570b96d89aF5E1B0Efefed", // DEGEN
  "0x0b3e328455c4059EEb9e3f84b5543F74E24e7E1b", // VIRTUAL
  "0x532f27101965dd16442E59d40670FaF5eBB142E4", // BRETT
  "0xAC1Bd2447a10150D01025454429261eD16f57b66", // TOSHI
];

async function main() {
  console.log("=================================================");
  console.log("ðŸš€ DESPLIEGUE: BaseUniversalArbitrage en Base L2");
  console.log("=================================================");

  const [deployer] = await ethers.getSigners();
  console.log("Deployer Address:", deployer.address);

  const balance = await ethers.provider.getBalance(deployer.address);
  console.log("Balance ETH nativo:", ethers.formatEther(balance), "ETH");

  if (balance === 0n) {
    throw new Error("âŒ Saldo insuficiente de ETH nativo para gas en Base.");
  }

  // 1. Desplegar Contrato
  console.log("\n1ï¸âƒ£ Desplegando BaseUniversalArbitrage...");
  const factory = await ethers.getContractFactory("BaseUniversalArbitrage");
  const contract = await factory.deploy();
  await contract.waitForDeployment();

  const contractAddress = await contract.getAddress();
  console.log("âœ… Contrato Desplegado Exitosamente!");
  console.log("ðŸ“ DirecciÃ³n:", contractAddress);
  console.log("ðŸ”— BaseScan: https://basescan.org/address/" + contractAddress);

  // 2. Pre-aprobar Tokens a Routers en Lotes PequeÃ±os (Gas Seguro)
  console.log("\n2ï¸âƒ£ Pre-aprobando routers para mÃ¡xima eficiencia de gas...");
  const batch1 = TOKENS_TO_APPROVE.slice(0, 3);
  const batch2 = TOKENS_TO_APPROVE.slice(3, 6);
  const batch3 = TOKENS_TO_APPROVE.slice(6, 9);

  for (const [idx, batch] of [batch1, batch2, batch3].entries()) {
    try {
      console.log("âš¡ Aprobando Lote #" + (idx + 1) + " (" + batch.length + " tokens)...");
      const tx = await contract.approveRouters(batch, { gasLimit: 350000n });
      await tx.wait(1);
      console.log("âœ… Lote #" + (idx + 1) + " aprobado (Tx: " + tx.hash + ")");
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.warn("âš ï¸ Advertencia Lote #" + (idx + 1) + ": " + err.message);
    }
  }

  // 3. Aprobar WETH de la wallet al nuevo contrato
  console.log("\n3ï¸âƒ£ Aprobando WETH de la wallet hacia el nuevo contrato...");
  try {
    const wethAbi = ["function approve(address spender, uint256 amount) external returns (bool)"];
    const weth = new ethers.Contract(TOKENS_TO_APPROVE[0], wethAbi, deployer);
    const appTx = await weth.approve(contractAddress, ethers.MaxUint256, { gasLimit: 80000n });
    await appTx.wait(1);
    console.log("âœ… WETH de la wallet aprobado al contrato (Tx: " + appTx.hash + ")");
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.warn("âš ï¸ Advertencia en aprobaciÃ³n de WETH:", err.message);
  }

  // 4. Actualizar .env local
  console.log("\n4ï¸âƒ£ Actualizando archivo .env...");
  const envPath = path.join(process.cwd(), ".env");
  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
    let envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
    if (envContent.includes("ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=")) {
      envContent = envContent.replace(/ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=.*/g, "ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=" + contractAddress);
    } else {
      envContent += "\nARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=" + contractAddress + "\n";
    }
    fs.writeFileSync(envPath, envContent, "utf8");
    console.log("âœ… .env actualizado con ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE =", contractAddress);
  }

  console.log("\nðŸŽ‰ DESPLIEGUE Y CONFIGURACIÃ“N COMPLETADOS CON Ã‰XITO!");
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error("âŒ Error en despliegue:", error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

FILE: C:\Users\Jose\Desktop\PELI_TRIAUDIO\flashloan-arbitrage-agent-v2\scripts\deploy_arb_ethers.js

const { ethers } = require('ethers');
const fs = require('fs');
const dotenv = require('dotenv');

dotenv.config();

const rpcs = [
  'https://arbitrum-one.publicnode.com',
  'https://1rpc.io/arb',
  'https://arb-mainnet.g.alchemy.com/v2/demo',
  'https://arb1.arbitrum.io/rpc'
];

async function deploy() {
  console.log('ðŸš€ Desplegando ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage con Ethers directo...');
  
  let provider = null;
  for (const url of rpcs) {
    try {
      const p = new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(url, 42161, { staticNetwork: true });
      await p.getBlockNumber();
      provider = p;
      console.log('âœ… Conectado a Arbitrum RPC:', url);
      break;
    } catch (e) {
      console.log('âŒ FallÃ³ RPC:', url, e.message);
    }
  }

  if (!provider) throw new Error('No se pudo conectar a ningÃºn RPC de Arbitrum.');

  const privKey = process.env.PRIVATE_KEY.startsWith('0x') ? process.env.PRIVATE_KEY : ('0x' + process.env.PRIVATE_KEY);
  const wallet = new ethers.Wallet(privKey, provider);
  console.log('Deployer Wallet:', wallet.address);

  const balance = await provider.getBalance(wallet.address);
  console.log('Saldo ETH en Arbitrum:', ethers.formatEther(balance), 'ETH');

  const artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('./artifacts/contracts/ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage.sol/ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage.json'));
  const factory = new ethers.ContractFactory(artifact.abi, artifact.bytecode, wallet);

  const feeData = await provider.getFeeData();
  console.log('Gas Price:', ethers.formatUnits(feeData.gasPrice || 0n, 'gwei'), 'gwei');

  console.log('Enviando transacciÃ³n de despliegue a Arbitrum One...');
  const contract = await factory.deploy({
    maxFeePerGas: (feeData.maxFeePerGas || feeData.gasPrice || 20000000n) * 110n / 100n,
    maxPriorityFeePerGas: feeData.maxPriorityFeePerGas || 0n
  });

  console.log('Tx Hash de despliegue:', contract.deploymentTransaction().hash);
  console.log('Esperando confirmaciÃ³n en Arbitrum...');
  await contract.waitForDeployment();

  const contractAddr = await contract.getAddress();
  console.log('\nðŸŽ‰ Â¡CONTRATO DESPLEGADO EXITOSAMENTE EN ARBITRUM ONE!');
  console.log('ðŸ“ DirecciÃ³n:', contractAddr);
  console.log('ðŸ”— Arbiscan: https://arbiscan.io/address/' + contractAddr);

  let envContent = fs.readFileSync('.env', 'utf8');
  if (!envContent.includes('ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_ARBITRUM=')) {
    envContent += `\nARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_ARBITRUM=${contractAddr}\n`;
    fs.writeFileSync('.env', envContent);
    console.log('âœ… DirecciÃ³n guardada en .env como ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_ARBITRUM');
  }
}

deploy().catch(console.error);

const { ethers } = require('ethers');
require('dotenv').config();

const ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE = process.env.ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE || '0xAD04bf95c85FF99aF80d885132B52A619C5F52BD';
const RPC_URL = 'https://developer-access-mainnet.base.org';

const ARBITRAGE_ABI = [
  'function executeSpotTriangular((address tokenA, address tokenB, address tokenC, uint8 dex1, uint8 dex2, uint8 dex3, uint24 feeOrSpacing1, uint24 feeOrSpacing2, uint24 feeOrSpacing3, uint256 minProfit) route, uint256 amountIn) external',
  'function executeFlashLoanTriangular((address tokenA, address tokenB, address tokenC, uint8 dex1, uint8 dex2, uint8 dex3, uint24 feeOrSpacing1, uint24 feeOrSpacing2, uint24 feeOrSpacing3, uint256 minProfit) route, uint256 loanAmount) external',
  'function owner() external view returns (address)'
];

const WETH = '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006';
const USDC = '0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913';
const AERO = '0x940181a94A35A4569E4529A3CDfB74e38FD98631';
const CBBTC = '0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf';
const DEGEN = '0x4ed4E862860beD51a9570b96d89aF5E1B0Efefed';
const VIRTUAL = '0x0b3e328455c4059EEb9e3f84b5543F74E24e7E1b';

const UNISWAP_V3_QUOTER_BASE = '0x3d4e44Eb1374240CE5F1B871ab261CD16335B76a';
const QUOTER_ABI = [
  'function quoteExactInputSingle((address tokenIn, address tokenOut, uint256 amountIn, uint24 fee, uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96)) external returns (uint256 amountOut, uint160 sqrtPriceX96After, uint32 initializedTicksCrossed, uint256 gasEstimate)'
];

async function runDiagnosis() {
  console.log('=== DIAGNÃ“STICO EN TIEMPO REAL: POR QUÃ‰ NO SE EJECUTAN Ã“RDENES ===\n');
  const provider = new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(RPC_URL, 8453);
  const formattedKey = process.env.PRIVATE_KEY.startsWith('0x') ? process.env.PRIVATE_KEY : ('0x' + process.env.PRIVATE_KEY);
  const wallet = new ethers.Wallet(formattedKey, provider);

  console.log('ðŸ‘¤ Wallet:', wallet.address);
  console.log('ðŸ“ Contrato Desplegado:', ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE);

  const contract = new ethers.Contract(ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE, ARBITRAGE_ABI, wallet);
  const owner = await contract.owner();
  console.log('ðŸ‘‘ Owner del Contrato:', owner);

  // 1. Probar simulaciÃ³n estÃ¡tica detallada de Spot $35 (0.014 WETH)
  const tradeAmountIn = ethers.parseEther('0.014');
  console.log('\n--- 1. PROBANDO SIMULACIÃ“N SPOT ($35 WETH) ---');
  
  const testRoutes = [
    { name: 'WETH âž” AERO âž” USDC âž” WETH', tokenB: AERO, tokenC: USDC, dex1: 0, dex2: 1, dex3: 0, fee1: 500, fee2: 200, fee3: 500 },
    { name: 'WETH âž” USDC âž” AERO âž” WETH', tokenB: USDC, tokenC: AERO, dex1: 0, dex2: 1, dex3: 0, fee1: 500, fee2: 200, fee3: 500 },
    { name: 'WETH âž” cbBTC âž” USDC âž” WETH', tokenB: CBBTC, tokenC: USDC, dex1: 0, dex2: 0, dex3: 0, fee1: 100, fee2: 500, fee3: 500 },
    { name: 'WETH âž” DEGEN âž” USDC âž” WETH', tokenB: DEGEN, tokenC: USDC, dex1: 0, dex2: 1, dex3: 0, fee1: 3000, fee2: 200, fee3: 500 },
    { name: 'WETH âž” VIRTUAL âž” USDC âž” WETH', tokenB: VIRTUAL, tokenC: USDC, dex1: 0, dex2: 1, dex3: 0, fee1: 3000, fee2: 200, fee3: 500 }
  ];

  for (const r of testRoutes) {
    const routeStruct = {
      tokenA: WETH,
      tokenB: r.tokenB,
      tokenC: r.tokenC,
      dex1: r.dex1,
      dex2: r.dex2,
      dex3: r.dex3,
      feeOrSpacing1: r.fee1,
      feeOrSpacing2: r.fee2,
      feeOrSpacing3: r.fee3,
      minProfit: 40_000_000_000n // ~$0.0001 USD
    };

    try {
      await contract.executeSpotTriangular.staticCall(routeStruct, tradeAmountIn);
      console.log(`âœ… [SPOT RENTABLE] ${r.name}: Â¡SimulaciÃ³n exitosa! DeberÃ­a ejecutar on-chain.`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.log(`âŒ [SPOT REVERTIDO] ${r.name}: ${err.reason || err.shortMessage || err.message}`);
    }
  }

  // 2. Probar simulaciÃ³n estÃ¡tica de Flash Loan ($5,000 USD / 2.0 WETH en Aave V3)
  console.log('\n--- 2. PROBANDO SIMULACIÃ“N FLASH LOAN (2.0 WETH / $5,000 USD AAVE V3) ---');
  const flashAmount = ethers.parseEther('2.0');
  for (const r of testRoutes) {
    const routeStruct = {
      tokenA: WETH,
      tokenB: r.tokenB,
      tokenC: r.tokenC,
      dex1: r.dex1,
      dex2: r.dex2,
      dex3: r.dex3,
      feeOrSpacing1: r.fee1,
      feeOrSpacing2: r.fee2,
      feeOrSpacing3: r.fee3,
      minProfit: 400_000_000_000n // ~$0.001 USD
    };

    try {
      await contract.executeFlashLoanTriangular.staticCall(routeStruct, flashAmount);
      console.log(`âœ… [FLASH LOAN RENTABLE] ${r.name}: Â¡SimulaciÃ³n exitosa!`);
    } catch (err) {
      console.log(`âŒ [FLASH LOAN REVERTIDO] ${r.name}: ${err.reason || err.shortMessage || err.message}`);
    }
  }

  // 3. Consulta de Quotes Directos en Uniswap V3 Quoter para calcular el Spread Exacto Actual
  console.log('\n--- 3. COTIZACIÃ“N MATEMÃTICA EXACTA ON-CHAIN (Uniswap V3 Quoter) ---');
  const quoter = new ethers.Contract(UNISWAP_V3_QUOTER_BASE, QUOTER_ABI, provider);

  try {
    // Swap 1: 0.014 WETH -> USDC (fee 500 = 0.05%)
    const q1 = await quoter.quoteExactInputSingle.staticCall({
      tokenIn: WETH,
      tokenOut: USDC,
      amountIn: tradeAmountIn,
      fee: 500,
      sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
    });
    const usdcOut = q1[0];
    console.log(`Paso 1: 0.014 WETH ($35.00) âž” ${ethers.formatUnits(usdcOut, 6)} USDC`);

    // Swap 2: USDC -> AERO (fee 500 = 0.05%)
    const q2 = await quoter.quoteExactInputSingle.staticCall({
      tokenIn: USDC,
      tokenOut: AERO,
      amountIn: usdcOut,
      fee: 500,
      sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
    });
    const aeroOut = q2[0];
    console.log(`Paso 2: ${ethers.formatUnits(usdcOut, 6)} USDC âž” ${ethers.formatUnits(aeroOut, 18)} AERO`);

    // Swap 3: AERO -> WETH (fee 500 = 0.05%)
    const q3 = await quoter.quoteExactInputSingle.staticCall({
      tokenIn: AERO,
      tokenOut: WETH,
      amountIn: aeroOut,
      fee: 500,
      sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
    });
    const wethFinal = q3[0];
    console.log(`Paso 3: ${ethers.formatUnits(aeroOut, 18)} AERO âž” ${ethers.formatEther(wethFinal)} WETH`);

    const netDiff = wethFinal - tradeAmountIn;
    const diffPct = (Number(netDiff) / Number(tradeAmountIn)) * 100;
    console.log(`\nðŸ“Š BALANCE DEL CICLO COMPLETO:`);
    console.log(`Entrada: ${ethers.formatEther(tradeAmountIn)} WETH ($35.00 USD)`);
    console.log(`Salida:  ${ethers.formatEther(wethFinal)} WETH ($${(parseFloat(ethers.formatEther(wethFinal)) * 2500).toFixed(4)} USD)`);
    console.log(`Diferencia Neta: ${ethers.formatEther(netDiff)} WETH (${diffPct.toFixed(3)}%)`);
    if (netDiff < 0n) {
      console.log(`âš ï¸ PÃ©rdida por comisiones de pool: -$${(Math.abs(parseFloat(ethers.formatEther(netDiff))) * 2500).toFixed(4)} USD.`);
      console.log(`ðŸ›¡ï¸ Por eso el contrato REVIERTE y bloquea la transacciÃ³n (protegiendo tus $35 intactos).`);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.log('Error al cotizar en Quoter:', err.message);
  }
}

runDiagnosis().catch(console.error);

require('dotenv').config();
const { Client } = require('ssh2');

const config = {
  host: process.env.VPS_HOST || '217.77.14.31',
  port: parseInt(process.env.VPS_PORT || '22', 10),
  username: process.env.VPS_USER || 'root',
  password: process.env.VPS_PASSWORD,
  readyTimeout: 20000,
};

console.log('ðŸš€ Iniciando arranque completo de arbitrage-cluster en VPS...');
const conn = new Client();

conn.on('ready', () => {
  console.log('âœ… SSH Conectado. Iniciando proceso...');
  const cmd = `
cd /root/4g3nt
ls -la
if [ ! -d "dist" ] || [ ! -f "dist/src/index.js" ]; then
  echo "Compilando TypeScript..."
  npm run build
fi
pm2 delete arbitrage-cluster || true
pm2 start dist/src/index.js --name "arbitrage-cluster" --max-memory-restart 4G --node-args="--max-old-space-size=4096"
pm2 save
sleep 4
echo "=== PM2 STATUS ==="
pm2 status
echo "=== PUERTO 3000 ==="
netstat -tuln | grep 3000 || ss -tuln | grep 3000
echo "=== PROBANDO HTTP LOCAL ==="
curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/status | head -n 30
`;

  conn.exec(cmd, (err, stream) => {
    if (err) {
      console.error('Error al ejecutar comando:', err);
      conn.end();
      return;
    }

    stream.on('data', (d) => process.stdout.write(d.toString()));
    stream.on('close', (code) => {
      console.log(`\nâœ… Proceso completado con cÃ³digo: ${code}`);
      conn.end();
      process.exit(0);
    });
  });
});

conn.on('error', (err) => {
  console.error('âŒ Error de conexiÃ³n SSH:', err.message);
  process.exit(1);
});

conn.connect(config);

require('dotenv').config();
const { Client } = require('ssh2');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const localBundle = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'update_bundle.tar.gz');
const remoteBundle = '/root/4g3nt/update_bundle.tar.gz';
const remoteBase = '/root/4g3nt';

const config = {
  host: process.env.VPS_HOST || '217.77.14.31',
  port: parseInt(process.env.VPS_PORT || '22', 10),
  username: process.env.VPS_USER || 'root',
  password: process.env.VPS_PASSWORD,
  readyTimeout: 30000,
};

console.log('ðŸš€ Conectando SSH al VPS...');
const conn = new Client();

conn.on('ready', () => {
  console.log('âœ… SSH Conectado. Abriendo SFTP...');
  conn.sftp((err, sftp) => {
    if (err) {
      console.error('SFTP Error:', err);
      conn.end();
      return;
    }

    console.log('ðŸ“¤ Subiendo update_bundle.tar.gz...');
    sftp.fastPut(localBundle, remoteBundle, (putErr) => {
      if (putErr) {
        console.error('Upload Error:', putErr);
        conn.end();
        return;
      }

      console.log('âœ… Bundle subido. Extrayendo y reiniciando PM2 en el VPS...');
      const cmd = `
cd ${remoteBase}
tar -xzf update_bundle.tar.gz
pm2 restart arbitrage-cluster || pm2 start dist/src/index.js --name "arbitrage-cluster" --max-memory-restart 4G --node-args="--max-old-space-size=4096"
sleep 4
echo "=== ESTADO PM2 ==="
pm2 status
echo "=== TELEMETRÃA DASHBOARD ==="
curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/status
`;

      conn.exec(cmd, (execErr, stream) => {
        if (execErr) {
          console.error('Exec error:', execErr);
          conn.end();
          return;
        }

        stream.on('data', (d) => process.stdout.write(d.toString()));
        stream.on('close', (code) => {
          console.log('\nðŸŽ‰ Â¡DESPLIEGUE Y REINICIO COMPLETADOS EXITOSAMENTE! (CÃ³digo:', code, ')');
          conn.end();
          process.exit(0);
        });
      });
    });
  });
}).on('error', (e) => {
  console.error('SSH Error:', e.message);
}).connect(config);

require('dotenv').config();
const { Client } = require('ssh2');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const localBundle = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'update_bundle.tar.gz');
const remoteBundle = '/root/4g3nt/update_bundle.tar.gz';
const remoteBase = '/root/4g3nt';

const config = {
  host: process.env.VPS_HOST || '217.77.14.31',
  port: parseInt(process.env.VPS_PORT || '22', 10),
  username: process.env.VPS_USER || 'root',
  password: process.env.VPS_PASSWORD,
  readyTimeout: 20000,
};

async function tryConnect(attempt = 1) {
  console.log(`ðŸš€ [Intento #${attempt}] Conectando SSH al VPS (217.77.14.31)...`);
  const conn = new Client();

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    conn.on('ready', () => {
      console.log('âœ… SSH Conectado exitosamente. Abriendo SFTP...');
      conn.sftp((err, sftp) => {
        if (err) {
          conn.end();
          return reject(err);
        }

        console.log('ðŸ“¤ Subiendo update_bundle.tar.gz...');
        sftp.fastPut(localBundle, remoteBundle, (putErr) => {
          if (putErr) {
            conn.end();
            return reject(putErr);
          }

          console.log('âœ… Bundle subido. Extrayendo y reiniciando PM2 en el VPS...');
          const cmd = `
cd ${remoteBase}
tar -xzf update_bundle.tar.gz
pm2 restart arbitrage-cluster || pm2 start dist/src/index.js --name "arbitrage-cluster" --max-memory-restart 4G --node-args="--max-old-space-size=4096"
sleep 4
echo "=== ESTADO PM2 ==="
pm2 status
echo "=== TELEMETRÃA DASHBOARD ==="
curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/status
`;

          conn.exec(cmd, (execErr, stream) => {
            if (execErr) {
              conn.end();
              return reject(execErr);
            }

            stream.on('data', (d) => process.stdout.write(d.toString()));
            stream.on('close', (code) => {
              console.log('\nðŸŽ‰ Â¡DESPLIEGUE Y REINICIO COMPLETADOS EXITOSAMENTE! (CÃ³digo:', code, ')');
              conn.end();
              resolve(true);
            });
          });
        });
      });
    });

    conn.on('error', (err) => {
      console.log(`âš ï¸ Intento #${attempt} fallÃ³:`, err.message);
      conn.end();
      reject(err);
    });

    conn.connect(config);
  });
}

async function main() {
  for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
    try {
      await tryConnect(i);
      process.exit(0);
    } catch (e) {
      console.log(`â³ Esperando 4 segundos antes del reintento #${i + 1}...`);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 4000));
    }
  }
}

main();
